B. Angabe eines fehlerhaften Steuersatzes). 11Eine<br>             Validierung der E-Rechnung ersetzt nicht die dem Empfänger obliegende Pflicht<br>             zur Überprüfung der Rechnung auf Vollständigkeit und Richtigkeit (vgl.<br>             Abschnitt 15.2a Abs. 6 und 15.11 Abs. 3), sondern unterstützt ihn hierbei. 12Ein<br>             Unternehmer kann sich bei Beachtung der Sorgfaltspflichten eines ordentlichen<br>             Kaufmanns auf das technische Ergebnis einer Validierung (hinsichtlich des<br>             Formats und der Geschäftsregeln) durch eine geeignete Validierungsanwendung<br>             verlassen. 13Zum Nachweis bietet es sich an, den Validierungsbericht<br>             aufzubewahren.“<br>            (5) 1Inländische Unternehmer müssen die technischen Voraussetzungen zum<br>         Empfang einer E-Rechnung schaffen. 2Dies gilt auch, wenn der Rechnungsempfänger<br>         der Sonderregelung nach § 19 UStG unterliegt. 3Bei einem Empfang mittels E-Mail ist<br>         kein gesondertes E-Mail-Postfach nur für den Empfang von E-Rechnungen<br>         erforderlich. 4Ist ein Unternehmer technisch nicht in der Lage, eine E-Rechnung<br>         empfangen zu können, bzw. verweigert er die Annahme, hat er kein Anrecht auf eine<br>         alternative Ausstellung einer sonstigen Rechnung durch den Rechnungsaussteller. 5In<br>         diesem Fall gelten die umsatzsteuerrechtlichen Pflichten des Rechnungsausstellers<br>         auch als erfüllt, wenn er eine E-Rechnung ausgestellt und sich nachweislich (z. B.<br>         anhand eines Sendeprotokolls) um eine ordnungsgemäße Übermittlung bemüht hat.“<br>Um den Austausch von E‑Rechnungen möglichst unkompliziert zu gestalten, sieht das Gesetz keinen bestimmten Weg vor, über den eine E‑Rechnung übermittelt werden muss. Dadurch wird die notwendige Flexibilität für verschiedene Lösungen in der Praxis erreicht. Daher kommen z. B. der Versand per E‑Mail, die Bereitstellung der Daten mittels einer elektronischen Schnittstelle, der gemeinsame Zugriff auf einen zentralen Speicherort innerhalb eines Konzernverbundes, die Übergabe z. B. auf einem USB‑Stick oder der Download über ein Internetportal in Betracht. Der Übermittlungsweg der E‑Rechnung im konkreten Einzelfall kann nur zivilrechtlich zwischen den Vertragsparteien geklärt werden.<br>Allerdings muss ein Unternehmen seit dem 1. Januar 2025 den Empfang einer E-Rechnung sicherstellen. Dazu reicht bereits ein E‑Mail‑Postfach aus.<br>Zum Seitenanfang<br>Nach oben<br>Zum Seitenanfang<br>9. In welchem Umfang muss eine E‑Rechnung eine elektronische Verarbeitung ermöglichen?<br>Aus der Definition, dass eine E‑Rechnung eine elektronische Verarbeitung ermöglichen muss, ergeben sich keine weitergehenden Verpflichtungen. Der Rechnungsempfänger ist nicht verpflichtet, tatsächlich eine weitergehende elektronische Verarbeitung durchzuführen, als dies ohnehin schon durch die Regelungen zur Aufbewahrung erforderlich ist (siehe hierzu Frage 13). Er kann durch eine elektronische Verarbeitung die Chancen der Digitalisierung nutzen, ist dazu aber nicht verpflichtet. | 2026-10-29 |

In einem dieser Dokumente sind das Entgelt und der darauf entfallende Steuerbetrag jeweils zusammengefasst anzugeben und alle anderen Dokumente zu bezeichnen, aus denen sich die übrigen Angaben nach § 14 Abs. 4 des Gesetzes ergeben. Die Angaben müssen leicht und eindeutig nachprüfbar sein.<br>(2) Den Anforderungen des § 14 Abs. 4 Satz 1 Nr. 1 des Gesetzes ist genügt, wenn sich auf Grund der in die Rechnung aufgenommenen Bezeichnungen der Name und die Anschrift sowohl des leistenden Unternehmers als auch des Leistungsempfängers eindeutig feststellen lassen.<br>(3) Für die in § 14 Abs. 4 Satz 1 Nr. 1 und 5 des Gesetzes vorgeschriebenen Angaben können Abkürzungen, Buchstaben, Zahlen oder Symbole verwendet werden, wenn ihre Bedeutung in der Rechnung oder in anderen Unterlagen eindeutig festgelegt ist. Die erforderlichen anderen Unterlagen müssen sowohl beim Aussteller als auch beim Empfänger der Rechnung vorhanden sein.<br>(4) Als Zeitpunkt der Lieferung oder sonstigen Leistung (§ 14 Abs. 4 Satz 1 Nr. 6 des Gesetzes) kann der Kalendermonat angegeben werden, in dem die Leistung ausgeführt wird.<br>(5) Eine Rechnung kann berichtigt werden, wenn a)<br>sie nicht alle Angaben nach § 14 Abs. 4 oder § 14a des Gesetzes enthält oder<br>b)<br>Angaben in der Rechnung unzutreffend sind.<br>Es müssen nur die fehlenden oder unzutreffenden Angaben durch ein Dokument, das spezifisch und eindeutig auf die Rechnung bezogen ist, übermittelt werden. Es gelten die gleichen Anforderungen an Form und Inhalt wie in § 14 des Gesetzes.<br>             „7Daher muss die Berichtigung einer E-Rechnung ebenfalls in der für diese<br>             vorgeschriebenen Form (unter Verwendung des entsprechenden Rechnungstyps)<br>             erfolgen. 8Eine Übermittlung der fehlenden oder unzutreffenden Angaben in<br>             einer anderen Form ist nicht ausreichend. 9Sofern dagegen für einen Umsatz nach<br>             § 14 Abs. 2 Satz 2 in Verbindung mit § 27 Abs. 38 UStG keine Verpflichtung zur<br>             Verwendung einer E-Rechnung besteht, kann eine Rechnungsberichtigung für<br>             diesen Umsatz auch ohne Verwendung einer E-Rechnung erfolgen.“ | 2026-10-29 |

Store the structured part unchanged, in the format received, for the period in § 14b(1) UStG, and keep it machine-readable for the tax office. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14b.html | UStG14b(1)–(4) retention/storage/access https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14b.html<br>BMF15October2025 UStAE formatretention https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 | (1) Der Unternehmer hat ein Doppel der Rechnung, die er selbst oder ein Dritter in seinem Namen und für seine Rechnung ausgestellt hat, sowie alle Rechnungen, die er erhalten oder die ein Leistungsempfänger oder in dessen Namen und für dessen Rechnung ein Dritter ausgestellt hat, acht Jahre aufzubewahren. Die Rechnungen müssen für den gesamten Zeitraum die Anforderungen des § 14 Absatz 3 Satz 1 erfüllen. Die Aufbewahrungsfrist beginnt mit dem Schluss des Kalenderjahres, in dem die Rechnung ausgestellt worden ist; § 147 Abs. 3 der Abgabenordnung bleibt unberührt. Die Sätze 1 bis 3 gelten auch 1.<br>für Fahrzeuglieferer (§ 2a);<br>2.<br>in den Fällen, in denen der letzte Abnehmer die Steuer nach § 13a Abs. 1 Nr. 5 schuldet, für den letzten Abnehmer;<br>3.<br>in den Fällen, in denen der Leistungsempfänger die Steuer nach § 13b Absatz 5 schuldet, für den Leistungsempfänger.<br>In den Fällen des § 14 Absatz 2 Satz 2 Nummer 3 hat der Leistungsempfänger die Rechnung, einen Zahlungsbeleg oder eine andere beweiskräftige Unterlage zwei Jahre gemäß den Sätzen 2 und 3 aufzubewahren, soweit er 1.<br>nicht Unternehmer ist oder<br>2.<br>Unternehmer ist, aber die Leistung für seinen nichtunternehmerischen Bereich verwendet.<br>(2) Der im Inland oder in einem der in § 1 Abs. 3 bezeichneten Gebiete ansässige Unternehmer hat alle Rechnungen im Inland oder in einem der in § 1 Abs. 3 bezeichneten Gebiete aufzubewahren. Handelt es sich um eine elektronische Aufbewahrung, die eine vollständige Fernabfrage (Online-Zugriff) der betreffenden Daten und deren Herunterladen und Verwendung gewährleistet, darf der Unternehmer die Rechnungen auch im übrigen Gemeinschaftsgebiet, in einem der in § 1 Abs. 3 bezeichneten Gebiete, im Gebiet von Büsingen oder auf der Insel Helgoland aufbewahren. Der Unternehmer hat dem Finanzamt den Aufbewahrungsort mitzuteilen, wenn er die Rechnungen nicht im Inland oder in einem der in § 1 Abs. 3 bezeichneten Gebiete aufbewahrt. Der nicht im Inland oder in einem der in § 1 Abs. 3 bezeichneten Gebiete ansässige Unternehmer hat den Aufbewahrungsort der nach Absatz 1 aufzubewahrenden Rechnungen im Gemeinschaftsgebiet, in den in § 1 Abs. 3 bezeichneten Gebieten, im Gebiet von Büsingen oder auf der Insel Helgoland zu bestimmen. In diesem Fall ist er verpflichtet, dem Finanzamt auf dessen Verlangen alle aufzubewahrenden Rechnungen und Daten oder die an deren Stelle tretenden Bild- und Datenträger unverzüglich zur Verfügung zu stellen. Kommt er dieser Verpflichtung nicht oder nicht rechtzeitig nach, kann das Finanzamt verlangen, dass er die Rechnungen im Inland oder in einem der in § 1 Abs. 3 bezeichneten Gebiete aufbewahrt.<br>(3) Ein im Inland oder in einem der in § 1 Abs. 3 bezeichneten Gebiete ansässiger Unternehmer ist ein Unternehmer, der in einem dieser Gebiete einen Wohnsitz, seinen Sitz, seine Geschäftsleitung oder eine Zweigniederlassung hat.<br>(4) Bewahrt ein Unternehmer die Rechnungen im übrigen Gemeinschaftsgebiet elektronisch auf, können die zuständigen Finanzbehörden die Rechnungen für Zwecke der Umsatzsteuerkontrolle über Online-Zugriff einsehen, herunterladen und verwenden. Es muss sichergestellt sein, dass die zuständigen Finanzbehörden die Rechnungen unverzüglich über Online-Zugriff einsehen, herunterladen und verwenden können.<br>(5) Will der Unternehmer die Rechnungen außerhalb des Gemeinschaftsgebiets elektronisch aufbewahren, gilt § 146 Absatz 2b der Abgabenordnung.<br>             „2Die Aufbewahrung einer Rechnung muss grundsätzlich im empfangenen<br>             Format erfolgen. 3Dabei muss die Echtheit der Herkunft, die Unversehrtheit ihres<br>             Inhalts und ihre Lesbarkeit gewährleistet sein (siehe Abschnitt 14.4). 4Bei einer<br>             E-Rechnung ist zumindest deren strukturierter Teil so aufzubewahren, dass er<br>             unversehrt in seiner ursprünglichen Form vorliegt. 5Für Zwecke der Umsatzsteuer<br>             gilt, dass alleine wegen einer Speicherung und Archivierung von E-Rechnungen<br>             außerhalb eines GoBD-konformen Datenverarbeitungssystems regelmäßig kein<br>             Verstoß gegen § 14b Abs. 1 UStG und die Unversehrtheit des Inhalts vorliegt. 6Zum<br>             Vorsteuerabzug aus einer sonstigen Rechnung in einem anderen elektronischen<br>             Format vgl. Abschnitt 15.2a Abs. 1.“ | 2026-10-29 |
| 532 | The method, step by step | 10. For a federal authority, follow the ERechV as well: XRechnung or an equal standard, Leitweg-ID, sign-up at the federal portal. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__4.html | ERechV4 standard/portal/rejection https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__4.html<br>ERechV5 supplementary content https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__5.html | (1) Für die Ausstellung von elektronischen Rechnungen haben Rechnungssteller und Rechnungssender grundsätzlich den Datenaustauschstandard XRechnung vom 29. September 2017 (BAnz AT 10.10.2017 B1) in der jeweils aktuellen Fassung zu verwenden. Es kann auch ein anderer Datenaustauschstandard verwendet werden, wenn er den Anforderungen der europäischen Norm für die elektronische Rechnungsstellung entspricht.<br>(2) Änderungen des Datenaustauschstandards XRechnung werden vom Bundesministerium des Innern, für Bau und Heimat im Bundesanzeiger bekannt gemacht. Bei jeder Änderung/Bekanntmachung ist das Herausgabedatum der Änderung/Bekanntmachung anzugeben und das Datum, ab dem der geänderte Datenaustauschstandard XRechnung anzuwenden ist.<br>(3) Für die Übermittlung von elektronischen Rechnungen haben Rechnungssteller und Rechnungssender ein Verwaltungsportal des Bundes im Sinne von § 2 Absatz 2 des Onlinezugangsgesetzes zu nutzen. Voraussetzung für die Übermittlung einer elektronischen Rechnung ist, dass der Rechnungssteller oder Rechnungssender sich zuvor mit einem Nutzerkonto im Sinne von § 2 Absatz 5 des Onlinezugangsgesetzes registriert. Elektronische Rechnungen, die über das Verwaltungsportal übermittelt werden, sind automationsunterstützt auf ihre formale Fehlerlosigkeit zu prüfen. Sobald die ordnungsgemäße Übermittlung einer elektronischen Rechnung festgestellt ist, ist der Rechnungssteller oder der Rechnungssender automationsunterstützt davon zu benachrichtigen. Eine formal fehlerhafte elektronische Rechnung ist automationsunterstützt abzulehnen. In diesem Fall ist der Rechnungssteller oder der Rechnungssender über die Ablehnung zu informieren.<br>(4) Erhält ein Rechnungsempfänger eine elektronische Rechnung, die keinem Nutzerkonto zugeordnet werden kann, so hat der Rechnungsempfänger die elektronische Rechnung abzulehnen. In diesem Fall erhalten die Rechnungssteller oder die Rechnungssender keine Information über die Ablehnung.<br>(5) Die Verpflichtungen nach den Absätzen 1 bis 4 gelten nicht für die Übermittlung von elektronischen Rechnungen, die in Verfahren der Organleihe nach § 159 Absatz 1 Nummer 5 des Gesetzes gegen Wettbewerbsbeschränkungen auszustellen sind.<br>(1) Die elektronische Rechnung hat neben den umsatzsteuerrechtlichen Rechnungsbestandteilen mindestens folgende Angaben zu enthalten: 1.<br>eine Leitweg-Identifikationsnummer,<br>2.<br>die Bankverbindungsdaten,<br>3.<br>die Zahlungsbedingungen und<br>4.<br>die De-Mail-Adresse oder eine E-Mail-Adresse des Rechnungsstellers.<br>(2) Die elektronische Rechnung hat zusätzlich zu den Angaben nach Absatz 1 folgende Angaben zu enthalten, wenn diese dem Rechnungssteller bereits bei Beauftragung übermittelt wurden: 1.<br>die Lieferantennummer,<br>2.<br>eine Bestellnummer.<br>(3) Die Vorgaben nach den Absätzen 1 und 2 gelten nicht für Rechnungen, die in Verfahren der Organleihe nach § 159 Absatz 1 Nummer 5 des Gesetzes gegen Wettbewerbsbeschränkungen auszustellen sind. | 2026-10-29 |
| 536 | Ask the client first | - Are you and your customer both established in Germany (seat, management, or a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply), and does the customer buy for its business? | — | intake question | — |
| 537 | Ask the client first | - When was the supply made, and when will the invoice be sent? The relief depends on both dates. | — | intake question | — |
| 538 | Ask the client first | - What was your total turnover (§ 19(2) UStG) in the previous calendar year, and are you a Kleinunternehmer? | — | intake question | — |
| 539 | Ask the client first | - What is the gross total of the invoice, and is any part of the supply exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG? | — | intake question | — |
| 540 | Ask the client first | - What does your software produce and read today: XRechnung, ZUGFeRD (which version and profile), EDI, or plain PDF? Can you receive, display and store an XML file? | — | intake question | — |
| 541 | Ask the client first | - Is the customer a public authority? If so, federal or state, and do you have its Leitweg-ID? | — | intake question | — |
| 545 | When to refuse or refer | - Invoices to state and local authorities: each federal state has its own rules, which this Guide does not cover. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 546 | When to refuse or refer | - Technical set-up: field mapping, validator messages, Peppol access, portal sign-up. Refer to the software vendor and to the KoSIT and FeRD material. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 547 | When to refuse or refer | - Whether a customer or supplier is "established" in Germany through a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply. Refer to a Steuerberater. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 548 | When to refuse or refer | - Whether a supply is exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG, or taxable by option. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 549 | When to refuse or refer | - A dispute over input tax where a paper or PDF invoice arrived although an e-invoice was due. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 550 | When to refuse or refer | - Fine proceedings and anything that may be tax evasion. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 551 | When to refuse or refer | - Disputes between the parties over format or channel: the ministry treats these as civil law. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 552 | When to refuse or refer | - Self-billing and the 2027 turnover test, VAT groups, and any case where it is unclear whose turnover counts. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 553 | When to refuse or refer | - An invoice sent after 31 December 2026 for a sale made in 2025 or 2026: the statute's wording gives no relief, and the pages read do not discuss it. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 554 | When to refuse or refer | - E-invoicing duties of other countries, and invoices under § 14(7) UStG that follow another member state's rules. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 555 | When to refuse or refer | - GoBD procedural documentation and the choice of an archive system. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |
| 556 | When to refuse or refer | - The planned reporting system: there is no law yet. | — | precise professional/source referral boundary | — |

| 62b7639a5a68:21 | When a German business must issue an e-invoice, when a paper or PDF invoice is still allowed, what every business must be able to receive, which formats count, how to correct, check and store e-invoices, and how invoicing federal authorities differs. It is for businesses, freelancers, landlords and associations in Germany, for foreign businesses that invoice German customers, and for the people who keep their books. Figures are for tax year 2026. The rules are read from the consolidated federal law pages (UStG, UStDV, AO, ERechV), from the finance ministry's letter of 15 October 2025, which writes the e-invoice rules into the VAT application decree (Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass), from the ministry's questions and answers page (as of March 2026), and from the GoBD change letters of 11 March 2024 and 14 July 2025. The reduced VAT rate is printed as a digit only in the ministry's tax booklet, 2025 edition; the statute states it in words. The ministry's first e-invoice letter, of 15 October 2024, is no longer at its address on the ministry's site, so nothing in this Guide rests on it alone. | Exact retained currentLF21, E-invoicing in Germany (E-Rechnung, XRechnung and ZUGFeRD) |
| 62b7639a5a68:25 | \| Field \| Value \| | Exact retained currentLF25, Section 1: Quick Reference |

| 62b7639a5a68:29 | \| What an e-invoice is \| An invoice that is issued, sent and received in a structured electronic format and that allows electronic processing (§ 14(1) UStG). Paper, a plain PDF, an image file or the text of an e-mail is an "other invoice" (sonstige Rechnung) \| | Exact retained currentLF29, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:30 | \| National CIUS \| XRechnung. The ministry says it meets the European standard EN 16931 and § 14(1) sentence 6 no. 1 UStG \| | Exact retained currentLF30, Section 1: Quick Reference |

See Section 3 \| | Quick reference currentLF29–42. | 62b7639a5a68:33 | \| Governing body \| Bundesministerium der Finanzen (BMF) for the VAT rules \| | Exact retained currentLF33, Section 1: Quick Reference |

| 62b7639a5a68:35 | \| Key legislation \| § 14, § 14b and § 27(38) UStG as changed by the Wachstumschancengesetz (Growth Opportunities Act); §§ 33, 34 and 34a UStDV; the E-Rechnungsverordnung (ERechV) for federal public contracts; GoBD (last changed 14 July 2025) \| | Exact retained currentLF35, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:36 | \| B2G portal \| OZG-RE (Onlinezugangsgesetz-konforme Rechnungseingangsplattform). The ministry's questions and answers page names it as the ERechV route. See Section 5 \| | Exact retained currentLF36, Section 1: Quick Reference |

| 62b7639a5a68:38 | \| B2B reception mandatory \| Since 1 January 2025, for every business in Germany, Kleinunternehmer included. No transition period \| | Exact retained currentLF38, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:39 | \| B2B issuance \| A duty in law since 1 January 2025. Relief for sales made in 2025 and 2026: every issuer, until 31 December 2026. Relief for sales made in 2027: only issuers within the turnover limit in Section 2, or EDI, until 31 December 2027. From 2028 no relief \| | Exact retained currentLF39, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:40 | \| B2C \| Not subject to the e-invoicing duty \| | Exact retained currentLF40, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:41 | \| Current status \| Invoicing federal authorities: in force. Receiving between businesses: in force. Issuing between businesses: in its transition \| | Exact retained currentLF41, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:42 | \| Guide version \| Refreshed against the official pages on 19 September 2026 \| | Quick reference currentLF29–42. Historical3.0.1andZUGFeRD2.1minimum narrowed to actualELSTERversion claim/currentKoSITconflict and2.0.1exclusion; no falsecurrentnormative3.0.1certification. |
| 62b7639a5a68:44 | **Five things older material gets wrong** | Exact retained currentLF44, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:46 | - **The duty to RECEIVE has no exceptions.** A Kleinunternehmer, a landlord of flats, a doctor and an association with a business side must all be able to receive e-invoices since 1 January 2025. Kleinunternehmer are released from ISSUING only. | Exact retained currentLF46, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:47 | - **Paper needs no consent.** During the transition a paper invoice may always be sent. Only an invoice in another electronic format, for example a plain PDF by e-mail, needs the recipient's consent. | Exact retained currentLF47, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:48 | - **An e-invoice to a German business needs no consent.** The recipient cannot insist on paper or PDF. | Exact retained currentLF48, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:49 | - **A plain PDF is not an e-invoice.** A hybrid ZUGFeRD file, a PDF that carries the structured data, can be one. | Exact retained currentLF49, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:50 | - **The turnover limit is a test of the ISSUER, for sales made in 2027 only.** It is not a phase that starts a duty. The duty exists since 2025; the limit decides who may still use the relief in 2027. | Exact retained currentLF50, Section 1: Quick Reference |
| 62b7639a5a68:56 | \| Scope \| Requirement \| | Exact retained currentLF56, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:58 | \| B2G (federal) \| Suppliers must issue and send invoices to federal contracting authorities in electronic form (§ 3(1) ERechV, in force since 27 November 2020). The standard is XRechnung, or another standard that meets the European norm (§ 4(1) ERechV). Invoices go through a federal administration portal, after sign-up for a user account (§ 4(3) ERechV). Not for invoices after a direct award up to the amount in the ERechV table below \| | Exact retained currentLF58, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:59 | \| B2G (Länder) \| The federal states have their own rules for invoices to state and local authorities. They are not on the pages read for this Guide. The legacy Guide said all of them were in force by 2024; that was not checked \| | Exact retained currentLF59, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:60 | \| B2B (reception) \| Every business established in Germany must be able to receive e-invoices since 1 January 2025. No exceptions \| | Exact retained currentLF60, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:61 | \| B2B (issuance) \| Duty under § 14(2) sentence 2 no. 1 UStG when supplier AND customer are both established in Germany and the supply is for the customer's business. Transition relief: see Transitional Rules below \| | Exact retained currentLF61, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:62 | \| B2C \| Not covered. An invoice to a consumer may be on paper. In an electronic format, e-invoice included, it needs the consumer's consent (§ 14(1) sentence 5 UStG) \| | Exact retained currentLF62, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:63 | \| Outside the duty to ISSUE an e-invoice \| Small invoices within the limit in the table below (§ 33 UStDV). Passenger tickets (§ 34 UStDV). Invoices issued by a Kleinunternehmer (§ 34a UStDV). Supplies exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG, for which there is no duty to invoice at all. Supplies to legal persons that are not businesses. Certain property-related services to consumers. Any supply where the supplier or the customer is not established in Germany \| | Exact retained currentLF63, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:65 | **The core rule**, all from § 14(1) and (2) UStG at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14.html | Exact retained currentLF65, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:67 | - **Duty to invoice.** For a supply to another business for its business, the supplier must issue an invoice "innerhalb von sechs Monaten nach Ausführung der Leistung" (within six months after the supply), unless the supply is exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG. | Exact retained currentLF67, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:68 | - **Duty to use an e-invoice.** That invoice must be an e-invoice when supplier and customer are both established in Germany (Inland) or in one of the areas named in § 1(3) UStG. | Exact retained currentLF68, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:69 | - **Established** means: seat, place of management, a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply, or, with no seat, the home or usual place of stay (§ 14(2) sentence 3 UStG). A foreign business that is only registered for VAT in Germany, with no fixed establishment here, may say so on its invoice to explain why it sends no e-invoice; the recipient may rely on that with a prudent merchant's care (ministry questions and answers, no. 3). | Exact retained currentLF69, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:70 | - **Business** is wide. The ministry's questions and answers page counts freelancers and people with exempt sales only, such as landlords of flats, Kleinunternehmer and doctors (no. 3). | Exact retained currentLF70, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:71 | - **Shorter deadlines stay.** § 14a UStG sets the fifteenth day of the following month for intra-Community supplies and for services under § 3a(2) UStG performed in another member state. See https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14a.html | Exact retained currentLF71, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:72 | - **Advance payments.** If money is received before the supply, the six months run from the receipt (decree section 14.1(3)). | Exact retained currentLF72, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:74 | **E-invoicing: turnover limit for the 2027 relief** | Exact retained currentLF74, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:76 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact retained currentLF76, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:78 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html \| | Exact retained currentLF78, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:79 | \| Total turnover (Gesamtumsatz, § 19(2) UStG) of the business that issues the invoice, in the previous calendar year: not more than \| EUR 800,000 \| § 27(38) no. 2 UStG: "im vorangegangenen Kalenderjahr nicht mehr als 800 000 Euro betragen hat" \| | Exact retained currentLF79, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:81 | - **What the limit is.** A cliff, tested on the issuer, never on the customer. At the limit or below, the relief for 2027 sales applies. One euro above, it does not, for any 2027 sale. | Exact retained currentLF81, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:82 | - **What counts.** Total turnover under § 19(2) UStG: supplies within the scope of German VAT (steuerbare Umsätze), counted on a receipts basis, less certain exempt supplies, without sales of fixed assets. See https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__19.html | Exact retained currentLF82, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:83 | - **Same number, other rules.** The same amount appears in § 141 AO (bookkeeping duty) and § 20 UStG (VAT on receipts). They are separate tests. | Exact retained currentLF83, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:85 | **Small invoices** | Exact retained currentLF85, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:87 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact retained currentLF76, Who Must Comply |

| 62b7639a5a68:90 | \| Invoice total up to which the invoice may always be an other invoice \| EUR 250 \| § 33 UStDV: "Eine Rechnung, deren Gesamtbetrag 250 Euro nicht übersteigt" \| | Exact retained currentLF90, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:92 | - **What the limit is.** The total of ONE invoice, VAT included. "Not more than": an invoice of exactly the amount in the table is still a small invoice. | Exact retained currentLF92, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:93 | - **The whole invoice counts.** If one invoice covers several supplies, only its total matters. Above the limit an e-invoice is due, even if the part that falls under the e-invoice duty is below it, for example because exempt or non-taxable items are billed on the same invoice (decree section 14.6(4)). | Exact retained currentLF93, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:94 | - **Not for every supply.** § 33 sentence 3 UStDV takes invoices for supplies under §§ 3c, 6a and 13b UStG (distance sales, intra-Community supplies, reverse charge) out of the small-invoice rule. | Exact retained currentLF94, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:95 | - **Cash purchases have no special rule.** A business meal in a restaurant or materials bought in a DIY store need an e-invoice once the invoice is above the limit, unless the issuer uses the transition relief. The ministry suggests a till receipt on the spot and an e-invoice by e-mail afterwards that corrects it (questions and answers, no. 10). | Exact retained currentLF95, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:97 | **The same two limits as the ministry prints them** | Exact retained currentLF97, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:99 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact retained currentLF76, Who Must Comply |

| 62b7639a5a68:114 | - **What a Kleinunternehmer may do.** Always issue an other invoice: paper, or with the recipient's consent another electronic format (§ 34a UStDV, decree section 14.7a(3)). Issuing an e-invoice is allowed too, and needs no consent when the customer is a German business. | Exact retained currentLF114, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:115 | - **What a Kleinunternehmer must do.** Be able to receive e-invoices (decree section 14.1(5)). | Exact retained currentLF115, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:116 | - **Leaving the scheme.** A Kleinunternehmer who moves to normal taxation must issue e-invoices from that moment, under the general conditions (questions and answers, no. 4). | Exact retained currentLF116, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:118 | **Federal public contracts (ERechV)** | Exact retained currentLF118, Who Must Comply |

| 62b7639a5a68:123 | \| No duty to invoice electronically for an invoice issued after a direct award (Direktauftrag) up to \| EUR 1,000 \| § 3(3) no. 1 ERechV: "die nach Erfüllung eines Direktauftrags bis zu einem Betrag von 1 000 Euro gestellt werden" \| | Exact retained currentLF123, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:125 | - **Other ERechV exceptions:** invoice data that must be kept secret (§ 8), the foreign service and other purchases abroad (§ 9), and Organleihe cases (§ 3(3) no. 3). | Exact retained currentLF125, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:126 | - **The ERechV does not say** whether its amount is net or gross. | Exact retained currentLF126, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:127 | - **Two sets of rules, side by side.** The ministry says there is no ranking between the UStG and the ERechV: each applies in its own field, and one can in practice narrow a choice the other leaves open (questions and answers, no. 4a). An invoice to a public body falls under the VAT e-invoice rules only when the body acts as a business. | Exact retained currentLF127, Who Must Comply |
| 62b7639a5a68:131 | All from the ministry's letter of 15 October 2025, decree section 14.1(4) and (5): https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 | Exact retained currentLF131, Receiving e-invoices |
| 62b7639a5a68:133 | - **The duty.** "Inländische Unternehmer müssen die technischen Voraussetzungen zum Empfang einer E-Rechnung schaffen." This also applies to a recipient under § 19 UStG. | Exact retained currentLF133, Receiving e-invoices |
| 62b7639a5a68:134 | - **No consent.** Issuing an e-invoice to another German business does not need the recipient's consent. | Exact retained currentLF134, Receiving e-invoices |
| 62b7639a5a68:135 | - **E-mail.** E-invoices may come by e-mail. The letter says: "Bei einem Empfang mittels E-Mail ist kein gesondertes E-Mail-Postfach nur für den Empfang von E-Rechnungen erforderlich." The ministry's questions and answers page goes one step further and says an e-mail inbox is already enough to receive (nos. 8 and 12). | Exact retained currentLF135, Receiving e-invoices |
| 62b7639a5a68:136 | - **Refusing does not help.** A business that cannot receive an e-invoice, or refuses it, has no right to an other invoice instead. The issuer's VAT duties then count as met if it issued an e-invoice and can show that it tried to send it properly, for example with a send log. | Exact retained currentLF136, Receiving e-invoices |
| 62b7639a5a68:137 | - **Receiving is not processing.** The recipient need not process the data electronically beyond what storage demands (questions and answers, no. 9). | Exact retained currentLF137, Receiving e-invoices |
| 62b7639a5a68:138 | - **Reading the file.** An XRechnung has no picture part; a viewer is needed. The tax administration offers one for XRechnung and ZUGFeRD files at https://www.elster.de/eportal/e-rechnung and calls it a non-binding service. | Exact retained currentLF138, Receiving e-invoices |
| 62b7639a5a68:139 | - **Associations.** An association must be able to receive e-invoices so far as it acts as a business. For supplies to its non-business side it need neither receive nor issue e-invoices (questions and answers, no. 5). | Exact retained currentLF139, Receiving e-invoices |

| 62b7639a5a68:164 | - **Two dates in each rule.** The statute ties each relief to when the supply was made AND to the day by which the invoice is sent. Read literally, an invoice sent in January 2027 for a sale made in December 2026 fits neither the first row (sent too late) nor the second (sale made too early). The pages read for this Guide do not settle that case. The ministry's questions and answers page (no. 11) describes the first relief the same way, as a period in which the invoice is issued, from 1 January 2025 to 31 December 2026. Invoice 2026 sales before the year ends, or refer. | Exact retained currentLF164, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) |
| 62b7639a5a68:165 | - **Issuing only.** § 27(38) UStG relieves issuers. There is no relief for receiving. | Exact retained currentLF165, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) |
| 62b7639a5a68:166 | - **The issuer decides.** The choice to use the relief lies with the issuer. The ministry adds that the rules leave a recipient free to pick a supplier that, for now, still issues paper invoices (questions and answers, no. 11). | Exact retained currentLF166, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) |
| 62b7639a5a68:167 | - **An other invoice still works while it is allowed.** As long as an other invoice may be issued, it remains a proper invoice for the input tax deduction (questions and answers, no. 2). | Exact retained currentLF167, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) |
| 62b7639a5a68:168 | - **Supplies before 2025** follow the old rules, even if invoiced later (questions and answers, no. 2). | Exact retained currentLF168, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) |
| 62b7639a5a68:169 | - **Payments on account.** Invoices for advance or part payments issued before the cut-off date need not be e-invoices, even if supply and payment come later (questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF169, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) |
| 62b7639a5a68:170 | - **Corrections during the transition** may still be made without an e-invoice (decree section 14.11(1); questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF170, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) |
| 62b7639a5a68:171 | - **After the transition an agreed format stays possible.** § 14(1) sentence 6 no. 2 UStG allows a structured format agreed between the parties, if the required data can be extracted correctly and completely into a format that meets the European standard or works with it. The ministry names EDI procedures such as EDIFACT as examples. | Exact retained currentLF171, Transitional Rules (2025-2027) |

The legacy Guide listed Peppol BIS Billing 3.0 here; that was not checked on an official page \| | Exact retained currentLF195, Accepted Formats |
| 62b7639a5a68:196 | \| Format agreed between issuer and recipient, for example EDI such as EDIFACT \| Structured \| May differ from EN 16931 \| Allowed if the data required by the UStG can be extracted correctly and completely into a format that meets EN 16931 or is interoperable with it (§ 14(1) sentence 6 no. 2 UStG) \| | Exact retained currentLF196, Accepted Formats |

| 62b7639a5a68:199 | - **Which allowed format is used** is a civil law question between the parties (decree section 14.1(12)). | Exact retained currentLF199, Accepted Formats |
| 62b7639a5a68:200 | - **Syntax names.** The ministry says only that the standard prescribes XML. KoSIT's specification names the same two syntaxes, UBL and the UN/CEFACT Cross Industry Invoice (CII). | Exact retained currentLF200, Accepted Formats |
| 62b7639a5a68:201 | - **Hybrid files: the XML leads.** Before 2025 the picture part led. Now the structured part is the leading part, and if the two differ the structured data decide (decree section 14.4(3); questions and answers, no. 12a). Input tax can be deducted only from the structured part. A picture part with different invoice details can count as a further (other) invoice, for which § 14c UStG must be checked: that section makes a person owe tax that is shown wrongly or without the right to show it (decree section 14c.1(4a)). Small technical differences, such as a shortened description or rounding differences, are not objected to. | Exact retained currentLF201, Accepted Formats |
| 62b7639a5a68:202 | - **Everything the VAT law demands must be in the structured part.** All details under §§ 14 and 14a UStG belong there. A mere reference in the data to an attachment that holds them in unstructured form is not enough. A link to an outside target is not enough. Extra detail, such as a timesheet as a PDF, may sit in an attachment inside the e-invoice (decree section 14.5(1) and (15)). | Exact retained currentLF202, Accepted Formats |

| 62b7639a5a68:292 | The ERechV demands a federal administration portal and a user account set up before the first invoice (§ 4(3) ERechV, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__4.html). A formally faulty invoice is rejected automatically and the sender is told. An invoice that cannot be matched to a user account is rejected without notice (§ 4(4) ERechV). The channels below are from the legacy Guide and from the federal e-invoicing information site named in the ministry's questions and answers page (e-rechnung-bund.de, run by the procurement office of the interior ministry). That site is not an allowed host for this Guide, so it is not linked, and the portal address in the legacy Guide was removed. | CurrentfederalrouteLF292–300. | 62b7639a5a68:334 | - Validation does not replace the recipient's own duty to check that the invoice is complete and correct. | Exact retained currentLF334, Section 6: Validation Rules |

| 62b7639a5a68:375 | \| Standard rate (Regelsteuersatz), on every taxable supply \| 19% \| § 12(1) UStG: "Die Steuer beträgt für jeden steuerpflichtigen Umsatz 19 Prozent der Bemessungsgrundlage" \| | Exact retained currentLF375, VAT Rates (2025/2026) |

| 62b7639a5a68:378 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact retained currentLF76, Who Must Comply |

| 62b7639a5a68:381 | \| Reduced rate (ermäßigter Steuersatz), only for the supplies listed in § 12(2) UStG and its annex \| 7% \| Tax booklet, 2025 edition: "den ermäßigten von 7 Prozent". The statute prints this rate in words \| | Exact retained currentLF381, VAT Rates (2025/2026) |
| 62b7639a5a68:383 | - **Correction.** The legacy Guide listed intra-EU supplies and exports under a zero rate. In German law they are EXEMPT under § 4 UStG, not taxed at a rate of zero. In the structured data they carry their own tax category code and an exemption reason. The only zero RATE in the statute is the one in the table. | Exact retained currentLF383, VAT Rates (2025/2026) |
| 62b7639a5a68:384 | - **Which goods and services take the reduced rate** is for the German VAT Guide, `germany-vat-return`. | Exact retained currentLF384, VAT Rates (2025/2026) |
| 62b7639a5a68:388 | These points describe the data format, not the tax law. The first point is carried over from the legacy Guide and was not checked. The others were checked on 19 September 2026 against KoSIT's XRechnung specification, version 3.0.2 (named, not linked). The specification of the format in use decides. | CurrentroundingLF388–395: actualR120 includes base quantity, line charges and allowances; Amount uses2decimals; BRCO17taxcategoryrule/BRS8grouping are separate. Oldonecenttoleranceandautomaticbankersruleunsupported; notpreservedascurrentlaw. |
| 62b7639a5a68:390 | - **Line-level rounding:** net amount = quantity × unit price, rounded to 2 decimal places | CurrentroundingLF388–395: actualR120 includes base quantity, line charges and allowances; Amount uses2decimals; BRCO17taxcategoryrule/BRS8grouping are separate. Oldonecenttoleranceandautomaticbankersruleunsupported; notpreservedascurrentlaw. |
| 62b7639a5a68:391 | - **Tax amount rounding:** the format has no tax amount per line. The tax is worked out once per VAT breakdown group (BG-23), that is per tax category and rate: the group's taxable amount (BT-116) times its rate (BT-119), divided by 100, rounded to two decimal places (rule BR-CO-17). The legacy Guide's tax amount per line was wrong | Exact retained currentLF391, Rounding |
| 62b7639a5a68:392 | - **Category totals:** for each tax category and each rate within it, the taxable amount (BT-116) must equal the sum of the line net amounts (BT-131) with that category and rate, plus document level charges, less document level allowances with that category and rate (rule BR-S-8 for category S) | Exact retained currentLF392, Rounding |
| 62b7639a5a68:393 | - **Validation tolerance:** the legacy Guide stated a one-cent tolerance per tax subtotal group. No allowed page prints it, so the amount was removed | Exact retained currentLF393, Rounding |
| 62b7639a5a68:394 | - **Rounding mode:** KoSIT's specification says only "gerundet auf zwei Dezimalstellen" and names no rounding mode. The legacy Guide's line on banker's rounding is not in it and was removed | Exact retained currentLF394, Rounding |
| 62b7639a5a68:395 | - **What the ministry says:** rounding differences between the picture part and the structured part of a hybrid invoice are not objected to (decree section 14c.1(4a)). | Exact retained currentLF395, Rounding |
| 62b7639a5a68:399 | Carried over from the legacy Guide (data format, not checked on an official page), except where a law or KoSIT's specification is named. | CurrentmultirateLF401–405. ReducedrateusesSnotAA; Zforbidsreason, E/AE/G/Orequireit; oldblanketallzero-taxreasoncorrected. |
| 62b7639a5a68:401 | - **Separate tax subtotal group per rate:** each VAT rate requires a separate tax subtotal group (BG-23). The law demands the net amount broken down by tax rate and by exemption (§ 14(4) no. 7 UStG) | Exact retained currentLF401, Multi-Rate Invoice Handling |
| 62b7639a5a68:402 | - **Tax category codes:** KoSIT's XRechnung specification allows only S (Standard rate), Z (Zero rated goods), E (Exempt from tax), AE (VAT Reverse Charge), K (intra-Community supply), G (export, tax not charged), O (outside scope of tax), and L and M for the Canary Islands and for Ceuta and Melilla. The legacy Guide's code AA for the reduced rate is not in that list. A reduced-rate line carries S with its own rate: rule BR-S-5 asks only that the rate under S be greater than zero | CurrentmultirateLF401–405. ReducedrateusesSnotAA; Zforbidsreason, E/AE/G/Orequireit; oldblanketallzero-taxreasoncorrected. |
| 62b7639a5a68:403 | - **Reverse charge category code:** for reverse charge (§ 13b UStG), use category code AE with a rate of zero. The invoice must carry the words "Steuerschuldnerschaft des Leistungsempfängers", and no tax is shown (§ 14a(5) UStG). The e-invoice duty applies to reverse-charge supplies between two German businesses (decree section 14.1(4)) | Exact retained currentLF403, Multi-Rate Invoice Handling |
| 62b7639a5a68:404 | - **Tax exemption reason population:** the tax exemption reason (BT-120/BT-121) must be given for the categories E, AE, G and O (rules BR-E-10, BR-AE-10, BR-G-10, BR-O-10) and must NOT be given for the zero rated category Z (rule BR-Z-10). The law demands a note that an exemption applies (§ 14(4) no. 8 UStG) | Exact retained currentLF404, Multi-Rate Invoice Handling |
| 62b7639a5a68:405 | - **Partly exempt invoices.** If only part of what is billed falls under the e-invoice duty, for example taxable and § 4 nos. 8 to 29 exempt items together, the whole invoice must be an e-invoice (decree section 14.1(4)) | Exact retained currentLF405, Multi-Rate Invoice Handling |
| 62b7639a5a68:409 | German VAT law (§ 14(4) UStG, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14.html) lists the content of an invoice. In an e-invoice all of it must be in the structured data: | Exact retained currentLF409, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:411 | 1. Full name and full address of the supplier and of the customer | Exact retained currentLF411, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:412 | 2. The supplier's tax number (Steuernummer) or VAT identification number (USt-IdNr.) | Exact retained currentLF412, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:413 | 3. Issue date | Exact retained currentLF413, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:414 | 4. A sequential invoice number, given once | Exact retained currentLF414, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:415 | 5. Quantity and kind (usual trade description) of the goods, or scope and kind of the service | Exact retained currentLF415, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:416 | 6. Time of the supply; for payments on account, the time of receipt if it is fixed and differs from the issue date | Exact retained currentLF416, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:417 | 7. The net amount broken down by tax rate and by exemption, and any reduction agreed in advance that is not yet in the price | Exact retained currentLF417, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:418 | 8. The tax rate and the tax amount, or a note that an exemption applies | Exact retained currentLF418, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:419 | 9. In the cases of § 14b(1) sentence 5 UStG, a note that the recipient must keep the invoice | Exact retained currentLF419, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:420 | 10. The word "Gutschrift" where the customer issues the invoice (self-billing) | Exact retained currentLF420, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:422 | - **Date of supply.** A phrase such as "Leistungsdatum entspricht Rechnungsdatum" is enough only on an other invoice. In an e-invoice the date must be in the structured field. A reference to a delivery note works only for other invoices (decree section 14.5(16)). The calendar month may be given as the time of supply, so several supplies of one month can go on one collective e-invoice (§ 31(4) UStDV; questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF422, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:423 | - **Cash discount.** A note such as the agreed discount terms is enough; the discount need not be shown as an amount (decree section 14.5(19)). | Exact retained currentLF423, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:424 | - **Small invoices, tickets and Kleinunternehmer invoices** need no sequential number (decree section 14.5(14)). | Exact retained currentLF424, § 14 UStG Invoice Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:428 | \| Requirement \| Detail \| | Exact retained currentLF428, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:430 | \| Retention period \| 8 years for a copy of every invoice issued and for every invoice received (§ 14b(1) UStG). The period starts at the end of the calendar year in which the invoice was issued. § 147(3) AO stays untouched: under it the period does not end while the documents still matter for a tax whose assessment period is open. Start of the eight-year period: it applies to every invoice whose old retention period had not yet run out on 31 December 2024; for banks, insurers and securities institutions only to invoices whose old period had not run out on 1 January 2026 (§ 27(40) UStG, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html) \| | Exact retained currentLF430, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:431 | \| Two-year duty for private recipients \| A consumer, or a business buying for its private side, who receives a taxable property-related service must keep the invoice, a payment record or other proof for two years (§ 14b(1) sentence 5 UStG) \| | Exact retained currentLF431, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:432 | \| Format \| Keep the invoice in the format in which it was received (decree section 14b.1(1); GoBD paragraph 131). For an e-invoice, at least the structured part must be kept so that it is unchanged and in its original form, and the tax office must be able to evaluate it by machine (decree section 14b.1(5)) \| | Exact retained currentLF432, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:433 | \| GoBD compliance \| The GoBD (ministry letter of 28 November 2019, changed on 11 March 2024 and 14 July 2025) apply for income tax and bookkeeping purposes. For VAT alone, the ministry says that storing e-invoices outside a GoBD-compliant system is as a rule ("regelmäßig") no breach of § 14b(1) UStG (decree section 14b.1(1)) \| | Exact retained currentLF433, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:434 | \| Hybrid invoices (ZUGFeRD) \| It is enough to keep the structured part. The human-readable part must be kept only if it holds extra or different information that matters for tax, for example booking notes or qualified electronic signatures (GoBD paragraphs 119 and 131). The XML must not be lost through a format change, for example to TIFF (GoBD paragraph 125) \| | Exact retained currentLF434, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:435 | \| Outgoing invoices \| With an invoicing program, no picture copy of the outgoing invoice (a PDF, or the PDF part of a hybrid file) needs to be stored, if an identical copy can be produced on request at any time (GoBD paragraph 76) \| | Exact retained currentLF435, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:436 | \| Content, not picture \| Vouchers received as structured data need to match in content only, not in appearance (GoBD paragraph 118) \| | Exact retained currentLF436, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:437 | \| Immutability \| Authenticity of origin, integrity of content and legibility must be secured for the whole period (§ 14b(1) sentence 2 with § 14(3) UStG). For an e-invoice, legible means machine-readable; a human-readable copy is not required (decree section 14.4(3)). A qualified electronic signature, if used, must be kept too (decree section 14b.1(6)) \| | Exact retained currentLF437, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:438 | \| Verfahrensdokumentation \| The legacy Guide says written procedural documentation of the e-invoice process (receipt, processing, archiving) is mandatory. That comes from the GoBD main letter of 28 November 2019, which is not among the pages read for this Guide. Carried over, not checked \| | Exact retained currentLF438, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:439 | \| Storage location \| In Germany. Electronic storage elsewhere in the EU is allowed if full online access, download and use of the data are secured; the tax office must then be told the place (§ 14b(2) and (4) UStG). Outside the EU only under § 146(2b) AO, which needs the tax office's approval on application (§ 14b(5) UStG) \| | Exact retained currentLF439, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:440 | \| Audit access \| Three forms under § 147(6) AO: the tax office inspects the stored data and uses the system itself (Z1, direct access); it demands that the data be evaluated by machine as it specifies (Z2, indirect access); or it demands that the data be handed over in a machine-readable format (Z3, data handover, for example on a data carrier or through a data exchange platform). The legacy Guide still called Z3 a data carrier handover; the GoBD change of 11 March 2024 renamed it \| | Exact retained currentLF440, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:441 | \| Conversion \| Converting an incoming electronic document to another format is allowed only under the conditions of GoBD paragraph 135 (GoBD paragraph 131). Paragraph 135 itself is in the main letter, which was not read. The legacy Guide's summary: the original must also be kept and the conversion documented \| | Exact retained currentLF441, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:442 | \| Validation report \| The ministry advises keeping it as proof (decree section 14.5(1)) \| | Exact retained currentLF442, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:444 | Sources for this section: § 14b UStG at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14b.html , § 147 AO at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__147.html , and the GoBD change letter of 14 July 2025 at https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Weitere_Steuerthemen/Abgabenordnung/2025-07-14-GoBD-2-aenderung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=4 | Exact retained currentLF444, Section 8: Archiving Requirements |
| 62b7639a5a68:448 | § 26a UStG prints its fines in words, not digits, so they are given in words here. See https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__26a.html | Exact retained currentLF448, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:450 | \| Violation \| Penalty \| | Exact retained currentLF450, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:452 | \| Not issuing an invoice, or not in time, against § 14(2) sentence 2 UStG, intentionally or recklessly (§ 26a(2) no. 1 UStG) \| Fine of up to five thousand euros ("bis zu fünftausend Euro", § 26a(3) UStG) \| | Exact retained currentLF452, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:453 | \| Not keeping a copy of an issued invoice or a received invoice for at least eight years, intentionally or recklessly (§ 26a(2) no. 2 UStG) \| Fine of up to five thousand euros \| | Exact retained currentLF453, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:454 | \| A private recipient not keeping the invoice or proof of a property-related service for at least two years, intentionally or recklessly (§ 26a(2) no. 3 UStG) \| Fine of up to one thousand euros ("bis zu tausend Euro") \| | Exact retained currentLF454, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:455 | \| Other invoice where an e-invoice was due: input VAT deduction \| The invoice is not a proper invoice under §§ 14 and 14a UStG, so in principle it gives no right to deduct input tax (decree section 15.2a(1)). The issuer can cure this by issuing an e-invoice afterwards that refers specifically and clearly to the first invoice (15.2a(7)). Without that, the decree's paragraph 15.2a(1a), on deducting input tax without a proper invoice, also applies to this case, and the ministry adds that a correct and complete other invoice will "regelmäßig" meet its conditions. The earlier sentences of 15.2a(1a), which set those conditions, are not in the letter and were not read \| | Exact retained currentLF455, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:456 | \| Estimated tax assessment \| The tax office estimates the tax base where books or records that must be kept cannot be presented (§ 162(2) AO) \| | Exact retained currentLF456, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:457 | \| GoBD non-compliance \| § 158 AO makes books and records the basis of taxation if they meet §§ 140 to 148 AO, and not so far as there is reason to doubt that they are correct. Where they are not taken as the basis, the tax office estimates (§ 162(2) AO). The legacy Guide's "safety margins" and "penalty surcharges" are not on the pages read and were removed \| | Exact retained currentLF457, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:458 | \| Repeated/wilful violations \| Tax evasion (Steuerhinterziehung) under § 370 AO for deliberate cases. Refer to a specialist \| | Exact retained currentLF458, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:460 | - **Correction.** The legacy Guide gave a fine of up to thirty thousand euros for "serious" retention breaches. The statute has no such case: that ceiling belongs to § 26a(1) UStG, which is about not paying VAT prepayments and similar amounts on time. It is not an invoicing fine. | Exact retained currentLF460, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:461 | - **Not settled on the pages read:** whether sending a paper or PDF invoice where an e-invoice was due counts as "not issuing" an invoice for the fine in the first row. The statute's wording is in the table. Refer. | Exact retained currentLF461, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:462 | - **No fault of the issuer.** A breach of the e-invoice duty is not held against an issuer who did not know, and with a prudent merchant's care could not have seen, that the customer bought as a business. Use of a VAT identification number or a business identification number (W-IdNr.) can be a sign that the customer is a business (decree section 14.1(6)). | Exact retained currentLF462, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:464 | **Refusing data access** | Exact retained currentLF464, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |

| 62b7639a5a68:472 | - **What it is.** Discretionary ("kann"). It also covers electronic books moved abroad without approval, and not moving them back on demand. | Exact retained currentLF472, Section 9: Penalties for Non-Compliance |
| 62b7639a5a68:478 | - E-invoices provide the data basis for the monthly or quarterly Umsatzsteuer-Voranmeldung (advance VAT return) via ELSTER. See the German VAT Guide, `germany-vat-return` | Exact retained currentLF478, VAT Return Integration |
| 62b7639a5a68:479 | - The input tax deduction needs an invoice issued under §§ 14 and 14a UStG (§ 15(1) no. 1 UStG, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__15.html). Where an e-invoice was due, only an e-invoice meets that in principle: see Section 9 | Exact retained currentLF479, VAT Return Integration |
| 62b7639a5a68:480 | - A content error (a missing or wrong detail under § 14(4) or § 14a UStG) means the invoice is not a proper one. Unless proof under decree paragraph 15.2a(1a) can be given, it must be corrected to open the right to deduct (decree section 15.2a(7)) | Exact retained currentLF480, VAT Return Integration |
| 62b7639a5a68:481 | - The legacy Guide spoke of plans for pre-filled VAT returns. The ministry's pages say something else: a system for reporting invoice data to the tax administration, transaction by transaction, is planned for a later date, and the e-invoice prepares it (questions and answers, no. 1). No law and no date yet | Exact retained currentLF481, VAT Return Integration |
| 62b7639a5a68:482 | - On request, e-invoices can be sent to the tax office through ELSTER, with the function "Belegnachreichung zur Steuererklärung" (questions and answers, no. 14): https://www.elster.de/eportal/formulare-leistungen/alleformulare/belegnachreichung | Exact retained currentLF482, VAT Return Integration |
| 62b7639a5a68:486 | - For Einzelunternehmer (sole proprietors) and Freiberufler (freelancers), e-invoice revenue data feeds into the Einkommensteuer return (Anlage S or Anlage G) | Exact retained currentLF486, Income Tax Integration |
| 62b7639a5a68:487 | - **Correction.** Under the EÜR (Einnahmenüberschussrechnung, cash basis, § 4(3) EStG) income counts in the calendar year in which it flows in (§ 11(1) EStG, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__11.html), not at the invoice date. The legacy Guide's advice to align e-invoice dates with payment dates was wrong: an invoice is dated when it is issued, and must be issued within six months of the supply | Exact retained currentLF487, Income Tax Integration |
| 62b7639a5a68:488 | - **Correction.** Under Bilanzierung (accruals basis) income is booked whatever the payment date, and profit counts when it is realised (§ 252(1) nos. 4 and 5 HGB, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/hgb/__252.html). The invoice date does not decide. See the bookkeeping Guide, `germany-bookkeeping` | Exact retained currentLF488, Income Tax Integration |
| 62b7639a5a68:492 | - An invoice to a business customer in another EU country is outside the German e-invoice duty, because the customer is not established in Germany. It may be an e-invoice if the customer agrees. The rules of § 14a UStG on content and the shorter deadline still apply | Exact retained currentLF492, Intra-EU Reporting |
| 62b7639a5a68:493 | - Intra-EU supplies reported via Zusammenfassende Meldung (recapitulative statement) must reconcile with the invoices issued for them (tax category K in the structured data) | Exact retained currentLF493, Intra-EU Reporting |
| 62b7639a5a68:494 | - Buyer VAT IDs on invoices (BT-48) should be checked against VIES | Exact retained currentLF494, Intra-EU Reporting |
| 62b7639a5a68:495 | - Exempt supplies under § 4 nos. 1 to 7 UStG between two German businesses, for example an intra-Community supply from Germany to another German business's fixed establishment elsewhere in the EU, DO need an e-invoice (decree section 14.1(4)) | Exact retained currentLF495, Intra-EU Reporting |
| 62b7639a5a68:499 | - Tax auditors may use Z1 (direct access), Z2 (machine evaluation by the taxpayer as the office specifies) or Z3 (data handover) for archived e-invoices: see Section 8 | Exact retained currentLF499, GoBD and Tax Audit |
| 62b7639a5a68:500 | - Verfahrensdokumentation must describe the e-invoice workflow from receipt to archiving (legacy statement, from the GoBD main letter, not checked) | Exact retained currentLF500, GoBD and Tax Audit |
| 62b7639a5a68:501 | - If the archive cannot be produced in an audit, the tax office can estimate (§ 162 AO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__162.html), and a delay payment can be set when data access is not granted: see Section 9 | Exact retained currentLF501, GoBD and Tax Audit |
| 62b7639a5a68:505 | All from the letter of 15 October 2025 (decree sections in brackets) at https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 or from the questions and answers page at https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/FAQ/e-rechnung.html | Exact retained currentLF505, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:507 | - **Self-billing (Gutschrift).** The e-invoice rules apply in the same way when the customer issues the invoice. If a self-billing document and an ordinary invoice cannot be combined in one e-invoice for technical reasons, separate e-invoices are issued (14.3(1) and (2)). | Exact retained currentLF507, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:508 | - **Special schemes.** The duty also covers supplies under the special rules of §§ 23a to 25c UStG (for example travel services and the margin scheme) and reverse-charge supplies under § 13b UStG between German businesses (14.1(4)). | Exact retained currentLF508, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:509 | - **Opted supplies.** A supply that is taxable only because of an option under § 9(1) UStG needs an e-invoice (14.1(4)). | Exact retained currentLF509, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:510 | - **Rent and other continuing contracts.** One e-invoice for the first part period is enough, if the contract is attached to it or the e-invoice otherwise makes clear that it is a standing invoice. When an invoice detail changes, for example a rent increase, a new e-invoice is due. A standing invoice that was properly issued as an other invoice needs no extra e-invoice as long as its details do not change (14.1(19), 14.5(17)). The invoice number may be a number from the contract, such as a flat or tenant number (14.5(12)). | Exact retained currentLF510, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:511 | - **Contracts as invoices.** A contract with all invoice details is an other invoice unless it meets the format rules. Where an e-invoice is due, an e-invoice must be issued in addition; the contract can sit in its attachment (14.1(17)). | Exact retained currentLF511, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:512 | - **Bank statements** that bill the bank's own service are invoices. They are e-invoices only if they meet the format and content rules (14.1(16)). | Exact retained currentLF512, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:513 | - **Inside one business.** Documents for movements between departments of the same business, or inside a VAT group (Organkreis), are internal vouchers, not invoices (14.1(1)). The e-invoice duty does not reach them. | Exact retained currentLF513, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:514 | - **Customer with a business and a private side.** For a legal person that buys for both sides, the e-invoice duty takes priority (14.1(8)). Where several persons buy jointly and businesses are among them, the invoice to that community must be an e-invoice once no transition relief applies (15.2a(3)). | Exact retained currentLF514, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:515 | - **Corrections.** Where an e-invoice was due, the correction must also be an e-invoice, using the invoice type for corrections. Sending the missing or wrong detail in another form is not enough (14.11(1)). Single line items may be corrected in a later e-invoice if it refers specifically and clearly to the first one (questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF515, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:516 | - **Price changes are not corrections.** Cash discounts, rebates and bonuses paid later, and price cuts after a complaint that leave the supply itself unchanged, change the tax base under § 17 UStG; the invoice need not be corrected. A change in what was supplied, such as changed quantities on a building invoice, does need a correction, at least of the description (14.11(4), 17.1(3a)). | Exact retained currentLF516, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:517 | - **Building work.** For now it is enough if the structured part lists the single trades with their sums, and a human-readable attachment, clearly referred to in the structured part, gives the detailed breakdown (questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF517, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:518 | - **Final invoices.** Part payments already received may be deducted in an unstructured attachment, if the structured part points to it expressly. The ministry says this can also be used after 31 December 2027 (questions and answers, no. 7b). | Exact retained currentLF518, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:519 | - **Fee notices of public bodies** that count as invoices follow the general rules, e-invoice duty included; one issued as an other invoice can be corrected by an e-invoice later (14.1(1)). | Exact retained currentLF519, Section 11: Cases the ministry has settled |
| 62b7639a5a68:523 | 1. Check whether an invoice is due at all. § 14(2) sentence 2 UStG demands one, within six months, for supplies to another business for its business, to a legal person that is not a business, and for taxable property-related work for anyone else. No duty for supplies exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14.html | Exact retained currentLF523, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:524 | 2. Check whether it must be an e-invoice. Yes, if supplier and customer are both established in Germany and the customer buys for its business (§ 14(2) sentence 2 no. 1 UStG). No, if one of them is established abroad, or the customer is a consumer or a legal person that is not a business. Same page as step 1. | Exact retained currentLF524, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:525 | 3. Check the lasting exceptions: small invoices (§ 33 UStDV), passenger tickets (§ 34 UStDV), invoices of a Kleinunternehmer (§ 34a UStDV). These may always be other invoices. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__33.html and https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__34.html and https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__34a.html | Exact retained currentLF525, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:526 | 4. Check the transition relief in § 27(38) UStG: the year of the supply, the day the invoice is sent and, for 2027 sales, the issuer's total turnover of the previous calendar year. If the relief applies and the issuer wants to use it: paper, or with the recipient's consent another electronic format. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__27.html | Exact retained currentLF526, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:527 | 5. If an e-invoice is due or wanted, pick an allowed format: XRechnung, ZUGFeRD from version 2.0.1 without MINIMUM and BASIC-WL, another format that meets the European standard, or an agreed format that allows full extraction. Put every detail of § 14(4) and § 14a UStG into the structured part. Ministry letter of 15 October 2025: https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Umsatzsteuer/Umsatzsteuer-Anwendungserlass/2025-10-15-einfuehrung-obligatorische-e-rechnung.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=5 | Exact retained currentLF527, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:528 | 6. Validate the file, keep the validation report, and send it by a channel agreed with the customer. The customer's consent to the e-invoice is not needed. Keep proof of sending. Same letter as step 5. | Exact retained currentLF528, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:529 | 7. On the receiving side: make sure e-invoices can be received, display the XML with a viewer (the tax administration offers one at https://www.elster.de/eportal/e-rechnung ), and check the invoice details before deducting input tax under § 15 UStG. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__15.html | Exact retained currentLF529, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:530 | 8. Correct a faulty invoice under § 31(5) UStDV with a document that refers specifically and clearly to it. Where an e-invoice was due, the correction is an e-invoice too. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustdv_1980/__31.html | Exact retained currentLF530, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:531 | 9. Store the structured part unchanged, in the format received, for the period in § 14b(1) UStG, and keep it machine-readable for the tax office. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ustg_1980/__14b.html | Exact retained currentLF531, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:532 | 10. For a federal authority, follow the ERechV as well: XRechnung or an equal standard, Leitweg-ID, sign-up at the federal portal. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/erechv/__4.html | Exact retained currentLF532, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:536 | - Are you and your customer both established in Germany (seat, management, or a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply), and does the customer buy for its business? | Exact retained currentLF536, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:537 | - When was the supply made, and when will the invoice be sent? The relief depends on both dates. | Exact retained currentLF537, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:538 | - What was your total turnover (§ 19(2) UStG) in the previous calendar year, and are you a Kleinunternehmer? | Exact retained currentLF538, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:539 | - What is the gross total of the invoice, and is any part of the supply exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG? | Exact retained currentLF539, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:540 | - What does your software produce and read today: XRechnung, ZUGFeRD (which version and profile), EDI, or plain PDF? Can you receive, display and store an XML file? | Exact retained currentLF540, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:541 | - Is the customer a public authority? If so, federal or state, and do you have its Leitweg-ID? | Exact retained currentLF541, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:545 | - Invoices to state and local authorities: each federal state has its own rules, which this Guide does not cover. | Exact retained currentLF545, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:546 | - Technical set-up: field mapping, validator messages, Peppol access, portal sign-up. Refer to the software vendor and to the KoSIT and FeRD material. | Exact retained currentLF546, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:547 | - Whether a customer or supplier is "established" in Germany through a fixed establishment that takes part in the supply. Refer to a Steuerberater. | Exact retained currentLF547, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:548 | - Whether a supply is exempt under § 4 nos. 8 to 29 UStG, or taxable by option. | Exact retained currentLF548, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:549 | - A dispute over input tax where a paper or PDF invoice arrived although an e-invoice was due. | Exact retained currentLF549, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:550 | - Fine proceedings and anything that may be tax evasion. | Exact retained currentLF550, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:551 | - Disputes between the parties over format or channel: the ministry treats these as civil law. | Exact retained currentLF551, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:552 | - Self-billing and the 2027 turnover test, VAT groups, and any case where it is unclear whose turnover counts. | Exact retained currentLF552, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:553 | - An invoice sent after 31 December 2026 for a sale made in 2025 or 2026: the statute's wording gives no relief, and the pages read do not discuss it. | Exact retained currentLF553, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:554 | - E-invoicing duties of other countries, and invoices under § 14(7) UStG that follow another member state's rules. | Exact retained currentLF554, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:555 | - GoBD procedural documentation and the choice of an archive system. | Exact retained currentLF555, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:556 | - The planned reporting system: there is no law yet. | Exact retained currentLF556, When to refuse or refer |

## Writer checks and limits

Full current Guide and actual first-introduction/main bodies read. Actual selected statutory, ministry, KoSIT, OASIS, FeRD and federal platform clauses are recorded using literal LF coordinates. Current ZIP acquisition is distinct from selected member reads and no validator execution is claimed. Exact namespace/profile/code/channel/layer gaps now have positive technical source quotes. Precise full Leitweg-pattern, three additional portal checks, main GoBD procedure/paragraph135, full UStAE15.2a(1a) and wrong-format fine limits remain explicit. Legacy since3.0.1 release attribution is not promoted into a fresh historical release check.
